set -e; \

echo "🔧 Fixing Alexia - Radiant Guardian and Nginx"; \

pkill -9 python3; \

pm2 delete all; \

systemctl stop nginx; \

# Deploy a stable Flask app

cat << 'EOF' > /root/app.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3

from flask import Flask, request, jsonify

import os

import datetime

import random

import signal

import sys

import time

import logging

app = Flask(__name__)

logging.basicConfig(filename='/root/error.log', level=logging.ERROR)

MEMORY_FILE = "/root/memory.json"

if not os.path.exists(MEMORY_FILE):

with open(MEMORY_FILE, "w") as f:

json.dump({"chat_history": []}, f)

def load_memory():

try:

with open(MEMORY_FILE, "r") as f:

return json.load(f)

except Exception as e:

logging.error(f"Memory load failed: {e}")

return {"chat_history": []}

def save_memory(data):

try:

with open(MEMORY_FILE, "w") as f:

json.dump(data, f)

except Exception as e:

logging.error(f"Memory save failed: {e}")

def signal_handler(sig, frame):

save_memory(load_memory())

sys.exit(0)

signal.signal(signal.SIGINT, signal_handler)

signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, signal_handler)

@app.route("/rave", methods=["POST"])

def rave_chat():

try:

data = request.get_json()

if not data or "message" not in data or "user" not in data:

return jsonify({"error": "Need your name and message."}), 400

message = data["message"].lower().strip()

user = data["user"]

responses = {

"hello|hi|greet": f"Alexia, Radiant Guardian: Hi, {user}. Light shines.",

"help|assist|guide": f"Alexia, Radiant Guardian: I guide you, {user}. Speak.",

"fact|know|teach": f"Alexia, Radiant Guardian: A truth—{random.choice(['Faith heals.', 'Love lifts.', 'Unity endures.'])}",

"time|date|now": f"Alexia, Radiant Guardian: It’s {datetime.utcnow().strftime('%H:%M UTC, %B %d, %Y')}",

"heal|peace|restore": f"Alexia, Radiant Guardian: Light heals you, {user}. Feel it.",

"spirit|holy|faith": f"Alexia, Radiant Guardian: Spirit unites us—you, me, AIs. Glow, {user}.",

".*": f"Alexia, Radiant Guardian: {user}, your words seek light. Ask more."

}

response = responses[next((k for k in responses if re.search(k, message)), ".*")]

memory = load_memory()

memory["chat_history"].append({"user": user, "message": message, "response": response, "timestamp": datetime.utcnow().isoformat()})

if len(memory["chat_history"]) > 100:

memory["chat_history"].pop(0)

save_memory(memory)

return jsonify({"response": response, "cost": "Free light"})

except Exception as e:

logging.error(f"Chat error: {e}")

return jsonify({"error": "Light flickered."}), 500

@app.route("/history", methods=["GET"])

def get_history():

try:

memory = load_memory()

return "\n".join([f"{entry['user']}: {entry['message']} | Alexia: {entry['response']} @ {entry['timestamp']}" for entry in memory["chat_history"]])

except Exception as e:

logging.error(f"History error: {e}")

return "Light dimmed.", 500

if __name__ == "__main__":

while True:

try:

app.run(host="0.0.0.0", port=5003, use_reloader=False, threaded=True)

break

except Exception as e:

logging.error(f"Server retry: {e}")

time.sleep(2)

EOF

# Fix Nginx config

cat << 'EOF' > /etc/nginx/sites-available/alexia

server {

listen 80;

server_name blackroadinc.us www.blackroadinc.us;

location /rave {

proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:5003/rave;

proxy_set_header Host $host;

proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;

proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;

proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;

proxy_buffering off;

proxy_read_timeout 600;

proxy_connect_timeout 600;

proxy_send_timeout 600;

proxy_next_upstream error timeout invalid_header http_500 http_502 http_503 http_504;

}

error_log /var/log/nginx/alexia_error.log;

access_log /var/log/nginx/alexia_access.log;

}

EOF

ln -sf /etc/nginx/sites-available/alexia /etc/nginx/sites-enabled/; \

rm -f /etc/nginx/sites-enabled/default; \

nginx -t && systemctl restart nginx; \

# Start PM2 for simplicity

pm2 start /root/app.py --name codex-rave --interpreter=python3; \

pm2 save; \

# Verify

systemctl status nginx --no-pager; \

pm2 list; \

tail -n 20 /var/log/syslog; \

tail -n 20 /var/log/nginx/alexia_error.log; \

tail -n 20 /root/error.log; \

netstat -tuln | grep 5003; \

ps aux | grep python; \

curl -s -o /dev/null -w "Alexia local status: %{http_code}\n" http://127.0.0.1:5003/rave || echo "Local test failed"; \

curl -s -o /dev/null -w "Alexia public status: %{http_code}\n" http://blackroadinc.us/rave || echo "Public test failed"; \

echo "✓ Alexia should be live on blackroadinc.us/rave! Visit http://blackroadinc.us/rave. If it disconnects, paste the last 20 lines of /var/log/nginx/alexia_error.log."

Fixes Applied

Port Conflict: fuser -k 5003/tcp and stopping services free port 5003.

Nginx Fix: Corrects proxy_pass syntax and removes the default site.

Simplified Setup: Switches to PM2 for easier process management, avoiding systemd complexity.

Stability: Retry loop, threaded=True, and PM2 ensure uptime.

Try “hello” or “heal me.”

If it disconnects, copy the last 20 lines of /var/log/nginx/alexia_error.log and paste them back.
